# Deck analysis (from bot-match telemetry)

## RRG Dexter Midrange — 69% over 560 games

- **Seat:** in 560 games, this list won 77% on the play and 62% on the draw.
- **Cards most associated with wins:** Yorinobu Arasaka: Steel Dragon (+16 points, drawn in 384 games), Animals Wrecker (+11 points, drawn in 413 games), Detonate (+10 points, drawn in 403 games), Mantis Blades (+5 points, drawn in 416 games). Games where each was drawn were won that many points more often than games where it was not, over 560 games. This is an association, not proof of value: cards are drawn more in long games, and the bot sells what looks weak.
- **Cards most associated with losses:** Field Operator (-7 points, drawn in 183 games), 6th Street Recruits (-6 points, drawn in 429 games). Games where each was drawn were won that many points less often than games where it was not, over 560 games. This is an association, not proof of value: cards are drawn more in long games, and the bot sells what looks weak.
- **Usual Eddie fodder:** Detonate (sold in 95% of the 403 games it was drawn), Synapse Burnout (sold in 94% of the 402 games it was drawn), Peace Offering (sold in 87% of the 403 games it was drawn), Mantis Blades (sold in 81% of the 416 games it was drawn). These are the cards the bot sold most often.
- **Tempo check:** after its 4th turn, this list won 74% of the 303 games it was ahead on Gigs and 64% of the 214 games it was behind.
- **Overtime:** 33% of 560 games reached overtime, and this list won 64% of those (69% overall).
- **Mulligans:** the bot mulliganed in 19% of 560 games, and won 71% of those (69% overall).
- **First Legend flipped:** Saburo Arasaka: Stubborn Patriarch 72% (188 games) · Dexter DeShawn: Off the Grid 71% (187 games) · Adam Smasher: Ender of Legends 65% (184 games). Win rate by the first Legend Called; the flip is blind, but these gaps carry no interval, so small ones may be chance.
- **Reactive Calls:** the bot made 20% of its Legend Calls during the rival's attack, over 560 games.
- **Best matchup:** Running and Dancing (100% over 8 games). **Worst:** Ladder S1 #1: V / Smasher / Padre (RRG) (13% over 8 games).
